Janiform Third-Party Notices
============================

This file inventories the non-workspace Cargo dependency closure for `crates/janiform-pybind`, which is the Rust extension packaged inside the Janiform wheel.
Python dependencies that are distributed separately from the Janiform wheel are not included here.
The inventory is intentionally conservative and may include build-support or macro-support crates
from that locked Cargo dependency closure in addition to code linked into the final extension.

found [here](https://github.com/golang/go/blob/b10849fbb97a2244c086991b4623ae9f32c212d0/src/strconv/extfloat.go).
